1,564 entries, set-status-complete to setup. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **set-status-complete**: The user wants to set the status of a plan folder to "complete". ([tylerdurrett/plotter](https://github.com/tylerdurrett/plotter/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/set-status-complete.md) · 2 copies)

- **set-target-function-role**: Map functions of a managed contract to the AccessManager role required to call them. ([EVMcrispr/evmcrispr](https://github.com/EVMcrispr/evmcrispr/blob/HEAD/modules/acl/src/commands/set-target-function-role.md) · ★33)
- **set-targets**: Save your KPIs, targets, budgets and known context so every analysis judges results against your goals ([supermetrics-public/claude-marketing-plugin](https://github.com/supermetrics-public/claude-marketing-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/supermetrics-marketing/commands/set-targets.md))
- **set-targets**: Set monthly KPI targets for a project - the numbers behind the Business Health panel. Every change previews first and needs your OK. ([trackian/claude-plugin](https://github.com/trackian/claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/set-targets.md))
- **set-task**: Set the active task for tracking and checkpoints ([victor-software-house/task-tracker-plugin](https://github.com/victor-software-house/task-tracker-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/set-task.md))
- **set-text**: Set a text record on an ENS name. ([EVMcrispr/evmcrispr](https://github.com/EVMcrispr/evmcrispr/blob/HEAD/modules/ens/src/commands/set-text.md) · ★33)
- **set-texture**: Define a quality of thought ([esmevane/oneiros](https://github.com/esmevane/oneiros/blob/HEAD/dist/commands/set-texture.md) · ★3)
- **set-theme**: Set project visual theme to: $ARGUMENTS Update CLAUDE.md with the chosen theme details from /themes/ folder. All subsequent agent calls will reference this active theme. Available Themes: corporate-precision - Wall Street meets Silicon Valley ([nicolewert/ai-buildathon-starter](https://github.com/nicolewert/ai-buildathon-starter/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/set-theme.md) · ★1 · 7 copies)
- **set-tier**: Change the tier (L1/L2/L3), the git policy or Deploys on push, with a proper walkthrough ([andrewsheerin/topographic-maps](https://github.com/andrewsheerin/topographic-maps/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/set-tier.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **set-times**: @../../.github/prompts/set-times.prompt.md ([enorm-labs/event-junkie](https://github.com/enorm-labs/event-junkie/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/set-times.md) · ★2)
- **set-title**: Update a feature's display title without renaming the slug ([awolve/open-claude-plugins](https://github.com/awolve/open-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/awolve-spec/commands/set-title.md) · 2 copies)
- **set-topic**: Bind the current directory to an existing wiki topic ([pietrbr/claude-plugins](https://github.com/pietrbr/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/llm-wiki/commands/set-topic.md))
- **set-trace-level**: Enable or disable TRACE log level on a SAP CPI iFlow to capture step payloads. Usage: /set-trace-level &lt;iflow-id&gt; &lt;TRACE|INFO|ERROR&gt; ([francismartinaldoe/CPI-Maestro](https://github.com/francismartinaldoe/CPI-Maestro/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/set-trace-level.md))
- **set-tracker**: Set the issue tracker for the current project. ([alinaqi/maggy](https://github.com/alinaqi/maggy/blob/HEAD/commands/set-tracker.md) · ★713 · 2 copies)
- **set-trait**: Set a specific profile field (e.g. /metame:set-trait status.focus "Learning Rust") ([Yaron9/MetaMe](https://github.com/Yaron9/MetaMe/blob/HEAD/plugin/commands/set-trait.md) · ★18)
- **set-units**: Set a member's share units in a GDA pool (admin only). Units are plain unitless weights: a member with 3 units earns 3x what a member with 1 unit earns. Setting 0 removes the member from future distributions. ([EVMcrispr/evmcrispr](https://github.com/EVMcrispr/evmcrispr/blob/HEAD/modules/superfluid/src/commands/set-units.md) · ★33)
- **set-up**: Bootstrap a new BA project workspace using MasterMind model_001 — scaffolds folder layout + drops CLAUDE.md onboarding + artifact templates ([giakhanh22024558/MasterMind](https://github.com/giakhanh22024558/MasterMind/blob/HEAD/models/model_001/setup/commands/set-up.md))
- **set-up**: Set up my longbook. Your longbook filled from your statements: every charge on record, the names you picked in setup filled in where your statements or your answers show them, each account's closing balance set, the charges that clearly repeat planned ahead, and the rest set to repeat once you confirm them. ([MXmaster2s/longbook-claude-plugin](https://github.com/MXmaster2s/longbook-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/longbook/commands/set-up.md) · 2 copies)
- **set-up-a-new-project**: Set up a new project. Set up a clean project for working with AI: structure, naming conventions, and a filled-in rules or context file. It asks for your conventions. (UX+AI, Productivity) ([IleanaMarcut/uxai-mcp-plugin](https://github.com/IleanaMarcut/uxai-mcp-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/uxai/commands/set-up-a-new-project.md) · ★2)
- **set-up-ai-for-kids**: Helps parents set up an AI assistant for a child with age-appropriate instructions, privacy settings, family rules and a first conversation about how AI works and when to come to an adult. ([hermes-hq/hodios-dist](https://github.com/hermes-hq/hodios-dist/blob/HEAD/native/opencode/.opencode/commands/set-up-ai-for-kids.md) · ★1)
- **set-up-alm-pipeline**: Set up a Power Platform ALM pipeline — source-control the unpacked solution tree, one build artifact promoted unchanged across dev→test→prod, connection references + environment variables + Key Vault secrets, managed solutions downstream, and a fresh-import smoke test before release. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/power-platform/commands/set-up-alm-pipeline.md) · ★9)

- **setup**: Full one-time project setup in one guided session — git preflight, then /intake (definition + security posture + stack) → /bootstrap (skeleton, proven) → /threat-model (before the gate, while the design is still cheap to change) → /gate (P1 review) → land + /wrapup, with a checkpoint commit per stage. - **setup**: Friendly one-time setup for telegram-notify. Walks you through creating a bot with @BotFather, then DISCOVERS your chat id automatically (message the bot, we read it off getUpdates) instead of making you hunt for it. Optionally names targets for different scenarios (alerts, deploys, a forum topic). Saves to a 0600 config file (~/.config/telegram-notify/env) that every command reads. Idempotent - safe to re-run to add a target or replace the token. ([Arbuzov/claude-loadout](https://github.com/Arbuzov/claude-loadout/blob/HEAD/plugins/telegram-notify/commands/setup.md) · ★1)
- **setup**: One-time setup for nvidia-council. Saves your council model list (and, for a self-hosted NIM, a base URL override) plus your already-exported NVIDIA_API_KEY to a 0600 config file (~/.config/nvidia-council/env) that the commands read automatically. No proxy and no base URL to configure - the endpoint defaults to NVIDIA's hosted https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1. For security the API key is never typed into chat: it must already be in this session's environment. A live env var still overrides the file. Idempotent. ([Arbuzov/claude-loadout](https://github.com/Arbuzov/claude-loadout/blob/HEAD/plugins/nvidia-council/commands/setup.md) · ★1)
- **setup**: Guided one-time setup for litellm-council. Collects your LITELLM_BASE_URL / LITELLM_COUNCIL_MODELS and saves them (with your already-exported LITELLM_API_KEY) to a 0600 config file (~/.config/litellm-council/env) that the commands read automatically. For security the API key is never typed into chat - it must already be in this Claude Code session's environment (exported before launch). A live env var still overrides the file. Idempotent. ([Arbuzov/claude-loadout](https://github.com/Arbuzov/claude-loadout/blob/HEAD/plugins/litellm-council/commands/setup.md) · ★1)
